Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents maintain external memory banks to support long-horizon interaction, yet most existing systems treat construction, retrieval, and utilization as isolated subroutines. This creates two coupled challenges: strategic blindness on the forward path of the memory cycle, where construction and retrieval are driven by local heuristics rather than explicit strategic reasoning, and sparse, delayed supervision on the backward path, where downstream failures rarely translate into direct repairs of the memory bank. To address these challenges, we propose MemMA, a plug-and-play multi-agent framework that coordinates the memory cycle along both the forward and backward paths. On the forward path, a Meta-Thinker produces structured guidance that steers a Memory Manager during construction and directs a Query Reasoner during iterative retrieval. On the backward path, MemMA introduces in-situ self-evolving memory construction, which synthesizes probe QA pairs, verifies the current memory, and converts failures into repair actions before the memory is finalized. Extensive experiments on LoCoMo show that MemMA consistently outperforms existing baselines across multiple LLM backbones and improves three different storage backends in a plug-and-play manner. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ventr1c/memma.

</details>


### [37] [Analysis Of Linguistic Stereotypes in Single and Multi-Agent Generative AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18729)
*Martina Ullasci,Marco Rondina,Riccardo Coppola,Flavio Giobergia,Riccardo Bellanca,Gabriele Mancari Pasi,Luca Prato,Federico Spinoso,Silvia Tagliente*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究复制了LLM输出中基于方言的刻板印象生成分析，并评估了提示工程和多智能体架构等缓解策略的有效性。研究发现SAE和AAE相关输出之间存在显著的刻板印象差异，Chain-of-Thought提示和批评-修订多智能体架构可有效缓解方言偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM输出存在方言偏见，特别是当同一输入以标准美式英语(SAE)和非裔美国人英语(AAE)提供时，LLM会产生基于方言的刻板印象推断。这种歧视性行为需要有效的缓解策略。

Method: 定义8个提示模板分析方言偏见的表现形式（如建议姓名、工作、形容词），使用LLM-as-judge方法评估输出偏见。测试了角色扮演提示、Chain-of-Thought提示和生成-批评-修订多智能体架构等缓解策略，评估了Claude Haiku和Phi-4 Mini等模型。

Result: 所有模板类别中都存在SAE和AAE相关输出之间的刻板印象差异，形容词和工作归因方面的效应最强。基线差异因模型而异，Claude Haiku的SAE-AAE差异最大，Phi-4 Mini最小。Chain-of-Thought提示对Claude Haiku有效，多智能体架构在所有模型中表现一致。

Conclusion: 建议在交叉性软件工程中进行公平性评估时，应包含针对特定模型的缓解策略验证，并在高影响力LLM部署中使用涉及批评模型的智能体架构进行工作流级控制。当前结果具有探索性，可通过扩大数据集规模和应用于不同语言/方言来扩展。

Abstract: Many works in the literature show that LLM outputs exhibit discriminatory behaviour, triggering stereotype-based inferences based on the dialect in which the inputs are written. This bias has been shown to be particularly pronounced when the same inputs are provided to LLMs in Standard American English (SAE) and African-American English (AAE). In this paper, we replicate existing analyses of dialect-sensitive stereotype generation in LLM outputs and investigate the effects of mitigation strategies, including prompt engineering (role-based and Chain-Of-Thought prompting) and multi-agent architectures composed of generate-critique-revise models. We define eight prompt templates to analyse different ways in which dialect bias can manifest, such as suggested names, jobs, and adjectives for SAE or AAE speakers. We use an LLM-as-judge approach to evaluate the bias in the results. Our results show that stereotype-bearing differences emerge between SAE- and AAE-related outputs across all template categories, with the strongest effects observed in adjective and job attribution. Baseline disparities vary substantially by model, with the largest SAE-AAE differential observed in Claude Haiku and the smallest in Phi-4 Mini. Chain-Of-Thought prompting proved to be an effective mitigation strategy for Claude Haiku, whereas the use of a multi-agent architecture ensured consistent mitigation across all the models. These findings suggest that for intersectionality-informed software engineering, fairness evaluation should include model-specific validation of mitigation strategies, and workflow-level controls (e.g., agentic architectures involving critique models) in high-impact LLM deployments. The current results are exploratory in nature and limited in scope, but can lead to extensions and replications by increasing the dataset size and applying the procedure to different languages or dialects.

</details>


### [38] [Memento-Skills: Let Agents Design Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18743)
*Huichi Zhou,Siyuan Guo,Anjie Liu,Zhongwei Yu,Ziqin Gong,Bowen Zhao,Zhixun Chen,Menglong Zhang,Yihang Chen,Jinsong Li,Runyu Yang,Qiangbin Liu,Xinlei Yu,Jianmin Zhou,Na Wang,Chunyang Sun,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Memento-Skills是一个通用、可持续学习的LLM智能体系统，充当"智能体设计智能体"的角色。它通过基于记忆的强化学习框架和状态提示词，利用可重用的技能（存储为结构化markdown文件）实现持续学习和能力提升，无需更新LLM参数即可适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于人工设计的智能体，缺乏一种能够端到端为新任务设计智能体的通用系统；同时需要实现持续学习而不必更新LLM参数。

Method: 构建于基于记忆的强化学习框架，使用状态提示词和结构化markdown文件存储可重用技能。引入Read-Write Reflective Learning机制：读取阶段使用行为可训练技能路由器根据当前状态选择最相关技能；写入阶段根据新经验更新扩展技能库。实现闭环设计，所有适应通过外部化技能和提示词的演变完成。

Result: 在General AI Assistants基准测试中实现26.2%的相对准确率提升；在Humanity's Last Exam中实现116.2%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: Memento-Skills使通用智能体能够为新任务端到端设计智能体。通过迭代技能生成和精炼，系统能够持续提升自身能力，无需更新底层LLLM参数即可实现持续学习和自我改进。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Memento-Skills}, a generalist, continually-learnable LLM agent system that functions as an \emph{agent-designing agent}: it autonomously constructs, adapts, and improves task-specific agents through experience. The system is built on a memory-based reinforcement learning framework with \emph{stateful prompts}, where reusable skills (stored as structured markdown files) serve as persistent, evolving memory. These skills encode both behaviour and context, enabling the agent to carry forward knowledge across interactions.
  Starting from simple elementary skills (like Web search and terminal operations), the agent continually improves via the \emph{Read--Write Reflective Learning} mechanism introduced in \emph{Memento~2}~\cite{wang2025memento2}. In the \emph{read} phase, a behaviour-trainable skill router selects the most relevant skill conditioned on the current stateful prompt; in the \emph{write} phase, the agent updates and expands its skill library based on new experience. This closed-loop design enables \emph{continual learning without updating LLM parameters}, as all adaptation is realised through the evolution of externalised skills and prompts.
  Unlike prior approaches that rely on human-designed agents, Memento-Skills enables a generalist agent to \emph{design agents end-to-end} for new tasks. Through iterative skill generation and refinement, the system progressively improves its own capabilities. Experiments on the \emph{General AI Assistants} benchmark and \emph{Humanity's Last Exam} demonstrate sustained gains, achieving 26.2\% and 116.2\% relative improvements in overall accuracy, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/Memento-Teams/Memento-Skills.

</details>


### [39] [NeuroGame Transformer: Gibbs-Inspired Attention Driven by Game Theory and Statistical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18761)
*Djamel Bouchaffra,Fayçal Ykhlef,Hanene Azzag,Mustapha Lebbah,Bilal Faye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroGame Transformer (NGT)，通过将注意力机制重新概念化为合作博弈和统计物理系统的双重视角，克服传统transformer中成对公式化的高阶依赖建模限制。NGT使用Shapley值和Banzhaf指数通过可学习门控参数组合形成外部磁场，同时用成对交互势捕获协同关系。系统能量遵循Ising哈密顿量，注意力权重作为Gibbs分布的边际概率出现。在SNLI上达到86.4%测试准确率，超越ALBERT-Base。  


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的成对公式化限制了token间高阶依赖的建模能力。传统注意力仅考虑两两之间的交互，无法有效捕获多个token之间的协同或竞争关系。需要一种新方法来建模更高阶的token交互。

Method: 提出NeuroGame Transformer (NGT)：1) 将token同时视为合作博弈中的玩家和统计物理中的自旋；2) 使用Shapley值（全局排列归因）和Banzhaf指数（局部联盟影响）通过可学习门控参数组合形成外部磁场；3) 成对交互势捕获协同关系；4) 系统能量遵循Ising哈密顿量，注意力权重作为Gibbs分布的边际概率通过平均场方程高效计算；5) 开发重要性加权Monte Carlo估计器避免显式指数因子确保数值稳定性。

Result: 在SNLI和MNLI-matched数据集上表现优异。SNLI测试准确率86.4%（验证集峰值86.6%），超越ALBERT-Base，与RoBERTa-Base高度竞争。优于部分主要高效transformer基线模型。

Conclusion: NGT通过博弈论和统计物理的融合成功建模了token间的高阶依赖关系。理论提供了收敛保证并刻画了由插值参数控制的公平性-敏感性权衡。实验验证了该方法的有效性，为高效transformer设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Standard attention mechanisms in transformers are limited by their pairwise formulation, which hinders the modeling of higher-order dependencies among tokens. We introduce the NeuroGame Transformer (NGT) to overcome this by reconceptualizing attention through a dual perspective: tokens are treated simultaneously as players in a cooperative game and as interacting spins in a statistical physics system. Token importance is quantified using two complementary game-theoretic concepts -- Shapley values for global, permutation-based attribution and Banzhaf indices for local, coalition-level influence. These are combined via a learnable gating parameter to form an external magnetic field, while pairwise interaction potentials capture synergistic relationships. The system's energy follows an Ising Hamiltonian, with attention weights emerging as marginal probabilities under the Gibbs distribution, efficiently computed via mean-field equations. To ensure scalability despite the exponential coalition space, we develop importance-weighted Monte Carlo estimators with Gibbs-distributed weights. This approach avoids explicit exponential factors, ensuring numerical stability for long sequences. We provide theoretical convergence guarantees and characterize the fairness-sensitivity trade-off governed by the interpolation parameter. Experimental results demonstrate that the NeuroGame Transformer achieves strong performance across SNLI, and MNLI-matched, outperforming some major efficient transformer baselines. On SNLI, it attains a test accuracy of 86.4\% (with a peak validation accuracy of 86.6\%), surpassing ALBERT-Base and remaining highly competitive with RoBERTa-Base. Code is available at https://github.com/dbouchaffra/NeuroGame-Transformer.

</details>


### [40] [A Concept is More Than a Word: Diversified Unlearning in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18767)
*Duc Hao Pham,Van Duy Truong,Duy Khanh Dinh,Tien Cuong Nguyen,Dien Hy Ngo,Tuan Anh Bui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diversified Unlearning 框架，通过用多样化提示集代替单一关键词来表示概念，以更精确、更稳健地从文本到图像扩散模型中擦除不良概念。该方法作为现有反学习管道的插件组件，在多个基准测试中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于关键词的概念反学习方法存在根本性局限：单一关键词仅代表概念的窄点估计，无法覆盖完整语义分布，且与相关概念在潜在空间中存在纠缠，导致擦除不彻底或过度遗忘问题。

Method: 提出 Diversified Unlearning 框架，通过多样化提示集（而非单一关键词）来表示待擦除概念。该方法：（1）构建上下文多样的提示集以覆盖概念的完整语义分布；（2）将框架作为插件集成到现有反学习流程中。

Result: 广泛实验表明：（1）更强的擦除效果；（2）更好地保留无关概念；（3）对对抗恢复攻击的鲁棒性显著提升。在多个基准和最先进的基线方法上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion:  Diversified Unlearning 通过使用多样化提示集表示概念，有效解决了关键词方法的局限性，作为通用插件可显著提升现有反学习管道的性能。

Abstract: Concept unlearning has emerged as a promising direction for reducing the risks of harmful content generation in text-to-image diffusion models by selectively erasing undesirable concepts from a model's parameters. Existing approaches typically rely on keywords to identify the target concept to be unlearned. However, we show that this keyword-based formulation is inherently limited: a visual concept is multi-dimensional, can be expressed in diverse textual forms, and often overlap with related concepts in the latent space, making keyword-only unlearning, which imprecisely indicate the target concept is brittle and prone to over-forgetting. This occurs because a single keyword represents only a narrow point estimate of the concept, failing to cover its full semantic distribution and entangled variations in the latent space. To address this limitation, we propose Diversified Unlearning, a distributional framework that represents a concept through a set of contextually diverse prompts rather than a single keyword. This richer representation enables more precise and robust unlearning. Through extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and state-of-the-art baselines, we demonstrate that integrating Diversified Unlearning as an add-on component into existing unlearning pipelines consistently achieves stronger erasure, better retention of unrelated concepts, and improved robustness against adversarial recovery attacks.

</details>

Abstract: This volume includes a selection of papers presented at the 2nd Workshop on Advancing Artificial Intelligence through Theory of Mind held at AAAI 2026 in Singapore on 26th January 2026. The purpose of this volume is to provide an open access and curated anthology for the ToM and AI research community.

### [42] [dTRPO: Trajectory Reduction in Policy Optimization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18806)
*Wenxuan Zhang,Lemeng Wu,Changsheng Zhao,Ernie Chang,Mingchen Zhuge,Zechun Liu,Andy Su,Hanxian Huang,Jun Chen,Chong Zhou,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Vikas Chandra,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Wei Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)的策略优化方法dTRPO，通过两种轨迹简化策略降低计算成本：1)利用新解遮令牌的概率比作为中间扩散状态的无偏估计；2)使用重遮蔽最终状态的單次前向传播估计完整轨迹概率。在7B dLLMs上的实验表明，该方法在STEM任务上提升达9.6%，编程任务达4.3%，指令跟随任务达3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLMs作为一种新的语言生成范式，在与人类偏好对齐方面面临新的挑战。离线策略训练中轨迹概率计算成本过高，限制了规模化训练。因此需要研究降低计算成本的有效方法。

Method: 提出两种轨迹简化策略：1)在参考策略正则化下，新解遮令牌的概率比是中间扩散状态的无偏估计；2)完整轨迹概率可通过重遮蔽最终状态的单一前向传播有效估计。将这两种策略整合到策略优化目标中，形成dTRPO方法。

Result: 在7B dLLMs的指令跟随和推理基准测试中，dTRPO显著提升了最先进的dLLMs性能：STEM任务提升达9.6%，编程任务达4.3%，指令跟随任务达3.0%。该方法具有离线、单次前向的强训练效率，且因输出质量高而具备改进的生成效率。

Conclusion: dTRPO通过轨迹简化策略有效降低了dLLMs策略优化的计算成本，使离线大规模训练成为可能，并显著提升了模型在STEM、编程和指令跟随任务上的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) introduce a new paradigm for language generation, which in turn presents new challenges for aligning them with human preferences. In this work, we aim to improve the policy optimization for dLLMs by reducing the cost of the trajectory probability calculation, thereby enabling scaled-up offline policy training. We prove that: (i) under reference policy regularization, the probability ratio of the newly unmasked tokens is an unbiased estimate of that of intermediate diffusion states, and (ii) the probability of the full trajectory can be effectively estimated with a single forward pass of a re-masked final state. By integrating these two trajectory reduction strategies into a policy optimization objective, we propose Trajectory Reduction Policy Optimization (dTRPO). We evaluate dTRPO on 7B dLLMs across instruction-following and reasoning benchmarks. Results show that it substantially improves the core performance of state-of-the-art dLLMs, achieving gains of up to 9.6% on STEM tasks, up to 4.3% on coding tasks, and up to 3.0% on instruction-following tasks. Moreover, dTRPO exhibits strong training efficiency due to its offline, single-forward nature, and achieves improved generation efficiency through high-quality outputs.

</details>

Abstract: This paper is the second one in a series of work on the mathematical creativity of LLM. In the first paper, the authors proposed three criteria for evaluating the mathematical creativity of LLM and constructed a benchmark dataset to measure it. This paper further explores the mathematical creativity of LLM, with a focus on investigating whether LLM can generate valuable and cutting-edge mathematical research problems. We develop an agent to generate unknown problems and produced 665 research problems in differential geometry. Through human verification, we find that many of these mathematical problems are unknown to experts and possess unique research value.

</details>


### [44] [ProRL Agent: Rollout-as-a-Service for RL Training of Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18815)
*Hao Zhang,Mingjie Liu,Shaokun Zhang,Songyang Han,Jian Hu,Zhenghui Jin,Yuchi Zhang,Shizhe Diao,Ximing Lu,Binfeng Xu,Zhiding Yu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProRL Agent是一种基于"rollout-as-a-service"理念的可扩展基础设施，通过API服务提供完整的智能体生命周期管理，并支持在无root HPC环境中运行多样化的智能体任务。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL训练基础设施将rollout编排与训练循环紧耦合，导致系统难以迁移和维护；同时RL训练需要在沙箱环境中生成大量 rollout轨迹，但现有系统缺乏标准化和可扩展性。

Conclusion: ProRL Agent是一个可扩展的开源基础设施，已集成到NVIDIA NeMo Gym中，能够有效支持多轮LLM智能体的RL训练。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents are increasingly important for solving complex, interactive tasks, and reinforcement learning (RL) is a key ingredient for improving their long-horizon behavior. However, RL training requires generating large numbers of sandboxed rollout trajectories, and existing infrastructures often couple rollout orchestration with the training loop, making systems hard to migrate and maintain. Under the rollout-as-a-service philosophy, we present ProRL Agent , a scalable infrastructure that serves the full agentic rollout lifecycle through an API service. ProRL Agent also provides standardized and extensible sandbox environments that support diverse agentic tasks in rootless HPC settings. We validate ProRL Agent through RL training on software engineering, math, STEM, and coding tasks. ProRL Agent is open-sourced and integrated as part of NVIDIA NeMo Gym.

</details>


### [45] [RewardFlow: Topology-Aware Reward Propagation on State Graphs for Agentic RL with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18859)
*Xiao Feng,Bo Han,Zhanke Zhou,Jiaqi Fan,Jiangchao Yao,Ka Ho Li,Dahai Yu,Michael Kwok-Po Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RewardFlow是一种轻量级方法，通过构建状态图利用推理轨迹的拓扑结构来估计状态级奖励，解决强化学习中稀疏奖励问题，显著提升LLM智能体推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型(LLM)智能体推理面临强化学习中终端奖励稀疏的难题，难以进行细粒度的状态级优化；而过程奖励建模需要训练专用奖励模型，计算成本高且扩展困难。

Method: RewardFlow通过构建状态图利用推理轨迹的内在拓扑结构，分析各状态对成功的贡献度，然后进行拓扑感知的图传播来量化贡献并生成客观的状态级奖励。

Result: RewardFlow作为密集奖励用于RL优化后，在四个智能体推理基准测试中显著优于之前的RL基线，表现出更优的性能、鲁棒性和训练效率。

Conclusion: RewardFlow提供了一种轻量级且高效的状态级奖励估计方法，有效解决了LLM智能体推理中稀疏奖励的挑战，为强化学习优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant promise for enhancing the agentic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) with external environments. However, the inherent sparsity of terminal rewards hinders fine-grained, state-level optimization. Although process reward modeling offers a promising alternative, training dedicated reward models often entails substantial computational costs and scaling difficulties. To address these challenges, we introduce RewardFlow, a lightweight method for estimating state-level rewards tailored to agentic reasoning tasks. RewardFlow leverages the intrinsic topological structure of states within reasoning trajectories by constructing state graphs. This enables an analysis of state-wise contributions to success, followed by topology-aware graph propagation to quantify contributions and yield objective, state-level rewards. When integrated as dense rewards for RL optimization, RewardFlow substantially outperforms prior RL baselines across four agentic reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance, robustness, and training efficiency. The implementation of RewardFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/RewardFlow.

</details>


### [46] [Conflict-Based Search for Multi Agent Path Finding with Asynchronous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18866)
*Xuemian Wu,Shizhe Zhao,Zhongqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体路径规划(MAPF)中的异步动作问题，提出了一种新的CBS-AA方法，解决了现有CCBS方法存在的不完整性问题。该方法具有完整性和最优性保证，实验表明可减少高达90%的分支数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAPF算法依赖同步动作假设（所有智能体同时开始动作且耗时一个时间单位），这限制了MAPF规划器在实际中的应用。现有的CCBS方法虽可处理异步动作的MAPF-AA问题，但因连续等待时间导致的不可数无限状态空间而被证明是不完整的。

Method: 提出Conflict-Based Search with Asynchronous Actions (CBS-AA)方法，基于CBS框架处理异步动作，并在其基础上开发了冲突解决技术以提高可扩展性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法可以减少高达90%的分支数。

Conclusion: CBS-AA方法绕过了CCBS的理论问题，在保证完整性和最优性的前提下有效解决了MAPF-AA问题，冲突解决技术显著提升了算法的可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) seeks collision-free paths for multiple agents from their respective start locations to their respective goal locations while minimizing path costs. Most existing MAPF algorithms rely on a common assumption of synchronized actions, where the actions of all agents start at the same time and always take a time unit, which may limit the use of MAPF planners in practice. To get rid of this assumption, Continuous-time Conflict-Based Search (CCBS) is a popular approach that can find optimal solutions for MAPF with asynchronous actions (MAPF-AA). However, CCBS has recently been identified to be incomplete due to an uncountably infinite state space created by continuous wait durations. This paper proposes a new method, Conflict-Based Search with Asynchronous Actions (CBS-AA), which bypasses this theoretical issue and can solve MAPF-AA with completeness and solution optimality guarantees. Based on CBS-AA, we also develop conflict resolution techniques to improve the scalability of CBS-AA further. Our test results show that our method can reduce the number of branches by up to 90%.

</details>


### [47] [Geography According to ChatGPT -- How Generative AI Represents and Reasons about Geography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18881)
*Krzysztof Janowicz,Gengchen Mai,Rui Zhu,Song Gao,Zhangyu Wang,Yingjie Hu,Lauren Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI系统如何表示和推理地理知识的问题，强调理解AI构建的"世界模型"与评估其准确性同等重要。作者提出三个探索性研究问题（vignettes）：(1)模型是否形成强默认值，以及对微小句法变化的敏感程度；(2)组合看似无害的任务是否会导致分布偏移（如使用AI创建人物画像）；(3)仅关注事实回忆是否忽略了对地理原则的更深层理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着公众越来越多地通过AI系统与地理空间互动，研究人员也日益依赖预训练模型，因此理解AI系统如何构建"世界"已成为关键问题。这不仅是评估准确性，更需要探究AI对地理的表征和推理能力。

Method: 通过三个说明性的探索性研究（vignettes）来展示研究问题：(1)测试模型对微小句法变化的敏感性；(2)检验组合任务是否产生分布偏移；(3)反思仅关注事实回忆是否忽视更深层理解。

Result: 本文为提出性问题（position paper），旨在引发讨论和后续研究，而非报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 呼吁学界关注AI地理表征研究，通过三个开放性问题为后续实证研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Understanding how AI will represent and reason about geography should be a key concern for all of us, as the broader public increasingly interacts with spaces and places through these systems. Similarly, in line with the nature of foundation models, our own research often relies on pre-trained models. Hence, understanding what world AI systems construct is as important as evaluating their accuracy, including factual recall. To motivate the need for such studies, we provide three illustrative vignettes, i.e., exploratory probes, in the hope that they will spark lively discussions and follow-up work: (1) Do models form strong defaults, and how brittle are model outputs to minute syntactic variations? (2) Can distributional shifts resurface from the composition of individually benign tasks, e.g., when using AI systems to create personas? (3) Do we overlook deeper questions of understanding when solely focusing on the ability of systems to recall facts such as geographic principles?

</details>


### [48] [Reasoning over mathematical objects: on-policy reward modeling and test time aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18886)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Seungone Kim,Ilia Kulikov,Jack Lanchantin,Xian Li,Tianjian Li,Bo Liu,Graham Neubig,Anaelia Ovalle,Swarnadeep Saha,Sainbayar Sukhbaatar,Sean Welleck,Jason Weston,Chenxi Whitehouse,Adina Williams,Jing Xu,Ping Yu,Weizhe Yuan,Jingyu Zhang,Wenting Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对当前语言模型评估过度依赖简化答案格式（数值、选择题）的问题，提出构建数学对象推导的基准测试和训练方法。发布了Principia套件，包含数学对象推导的训练数据和基准，并提出基于LLM判断器和验证器的训练配方，验证了策略内判断器训练和测试时计算扩展的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前STEM领域（数学、物理、化学）的推理任务需要最终输出形式化的数学表达式，但现有LM评估主要使用简化的答案格式（数值、选择题），无法满足实际应用需求。这导致了评估与真实应用场景之间的差距。

Method: 构建Principia基准测试套件；开发基于LLM判断器和验证器的训练配方；探索策略内(on-policy)判断器训练提升性能；利用策略内训练在测试时通过聚合扩展计算能力。

Result: 强大的语言模型如Qwen3-235B和o3在Principia上表现困难；所提出的训练配方在不同LLM backbone上都能带来显著性能提升；同时在现有数值推理和MCQA任务上也取得改进，展示了推理能力的跨格式泛化。

Conclusion: 通过构建专门的数学对象推导基准和训练方法，可以有效提升语言模型在形式化数学表达式推理方面的能力，且这种改进具有跨格式泛化特性，能够同时提升数值和选择题任务的表现。

Abstract: The ability to precisely derive mathematical objects is a core requirement for downstream STEM applications, including mathematics, physics, and chemistry, where reasoning must culminate in formally structured expressions. Yet, current LM evaluations of mathematical and scientific reasoning rely heavily on simplified answer formats such as numerical values or multiple choice options due to the convenience of automated assessment. In this paper we provide three contributions for improving reasoning over mathematical objects: (i) we build and release training data and benchmarks for deriving mathematical objects, the Principia suite; (ii) we provide training recipes with strong LLM-judges and verifiers, where we show that on-policy judge training boosts performance; (iii) we show how on-policy training can also be used to scale test-time compute via aggregation. We find that strong LMs such as Qwen3-235B and o3 struggle on Principia, while our training recipes can bring significant improvements over different LLM backbones, while simultaneously improving results on existing numerical and MCQA tasks, demonstrating cross-format generalization of reasoning abilities.

</details>


### [49] [Quantitative Introspection in Language Models: Tracking Internal States Across Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18893)
*Nicolas Martorell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探索使用大型语言模型的数值自我报告来追踪其内部情绪状态，发现logit-based自我报告可有效追踪探针定义的情绪状态，且随模型规模提升性能。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如线性探针）在追踪LLM内部状态方面存在局限：白盒方法对高维表征的压缩不完美，且随模型增大应用困难。需要一种更简便的内部状态追踪方法。

Method: 研究四对概念（幸福感、兴趣、专注度、冲动性），在40个十轮对话中，将自我报告与概念匹配的探针定义内部状态之间的因果信息耦合定义为内省能力，比较greedy解码和logit-based自我报告的效果，并使用激活 steering 验证因果关系。

Result: Greedy解码的自我报告会崩溃为少数无信息量值；而logit-based自我报告可追踪可解释的内部状态（LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct: Spearman ρ=0.40-0.76，isotonic R²=0.12-0.54），并随对话演进。内省能力在第1轮存在但会演化，通过概念 steering 可选择性提升（R²提升达0.30）。性能随模型规模提升（LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct中R²≈0.93），并在其他模型家族中部分复现。

Conclusion: 数值自我报告是一种可行且有价值的补充工具，可用于追踪对话AI系统中的内部情绪状态，且在某些情况下随模型增大性能提升。

Abstract: Tracking the internal states of large language models across conversations is important for safety, interpretability, and model welfare, yet current methods are limited. Linear probes and other white-box methods compress high-dimensional representations imperfectly and are harder to apply with increasing model size. Taking inspiration from human psychology, where numeric self-report is a widely used tool for tracking internal states, we ask whether LLMs' own numeric self-reports can track probe-defined emotive states over time. We study four concept pairs (wellbeing, interest, focus, and impulsivity) in 40 ten-turn conversations, operationalizing introspection as the causal informational coupling between a model's self-report and a concept-matched probe-defined internal state. We find that greedy-decoded self-reports collapse outputs to few uninformative values, but introspective capacity can be unmasked by calculating logit-based self-reports. This metric tracks interpretable internal states (Spearman $ρ= 0.40$-$0.76$; isotonic $R^2 = 0.12$-$0.54$ in LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct), follows how those states change over time, and activation steering confirms the coupling is causal. Furthermore, we find that introspection is present at turn 1 but evolves through conversation, and can be selectively improved by steering along one concept to boost introspection for another ($ΔR^2$ up to $0.30$). Crucially, these phenomena scale with model size in some cases, approaching $R^2 \approx 0.93$ in LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, and partially replicate in other model families. Together, these results position numeric self-report as a viable, complementary tool for tracking internal emotive states in conversational AI systems.

</details>


### [50] [Secure Linear Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18908)
*Matt Gorbett,Suman Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个隐私保护框架，利用独立训练语言模型之间的表征趋同性，实现跨模型推理。通过在共享公共数据集上学习仿射变换，并结合同态加密保护客户端查询，可在保证安全性的同时实现亚秒级推理延迟。实验表明，线性对齐在嵌入分类、分布外检测等任务上性能损失极小，并首次实现了独立训练模型之间的文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 语言模型尽管训练目标、架构和数据模态不同，但倾向于学习相似的表征；2. 这种表征兼容性为跨模型对齐提供了新机会；3. 同时也为安全、隐私或竞争约束下禁止直接数据或模型共享的场景提供了新应用前景

Method: 1. 利用独立模型最终隐藏状态学习线性变换（仿射变换）；2. 在共享公共数据集上训练对齐模型；3. 应用同态加密仅加密线性对齐和分类操作；4. 实现跨模型的嵌入分类、分布外检测和文本生成

Result: 1. 跨模型映射在嵌入分类和分布外检测任务上性能 degradation 极小；2. 首次发现线性对齐可实现独立训练模型之间的文本生成；3. 在保持强安全性的同时实现亚秒级推理延迟

Conclusion: 独立训练的语言模型之间存在显著的表征趋同性，这使得跨模型推理成为可能。该隐私保护框架在保护客户端查询的同时，能够以极低延迟实现有效的跨模型任务迁移，为隐私敏感场景下的模型协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Language models increasingly appear to learn similar representations, despite differences in training objectives, architectures, and data modalities. This emerging compatibility between independently trained models introduces new opportunities for cross-model alignment to downstream objectives. Moreover, it unlocks new potential application domains, such as settings where security, privacy, or competitive constraints prohibit direct data or model sharing. In this work, we propose a privacy-preserving framework that exploits representational convergence to enable cross-silo inference between independent language models. The framework learns an affine transformation over a shared public dataset and applies homomorphic encryption to protect client queries during inference. By encrypting only the linear alignment and classification operations, the method achieves sub-second inference latency while maintaining strong security guarantees. We support this framework with an empirical investigation into representational convergence, in which we learn linear transformations between the final hidden states of independent models. We evaluate these cross-model mappings on embedding classification and out-of-distribution detection, observing minimal performance degradation across model pairs. Additionally, we show for the first time that linear alignment sometimes enables text generation across independently trained models.

</details>


### [51] [Teleological Inference in Structural Causal Models via Intentional Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18968)
*Dario Compagno,Fabio Massimo Zennaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将结构因果模型(SCM)从因果问题扩展到目的论问题，提出"意向性干预"算子和"结构最终模型(SFM)"，用于建模具有目标导向的智能体及其意图，并实现对智能体的检测和意图发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前方法在建模具有目标导向的智能体方面存在局限性，无法有效处理智能体对因果系统进行干预以实现特定目标的问题，因此需要新的形式化框架来回答关于智能体意图的目的论问题。

Method: 引入时间无关的意向性干预算子，生成孪生SCM称为结构最终模型(SFM)。SFM将观察值视为意向性干预的结果，并将其与反事实条件（若智能体未干预会发生什么）相关联。

Result: SFM框架可用于实证检测智能体的存在，并发现其意图。

Conclusion: SCM不仅适用于因果推理，还可扩展到目的论问题的建模，为理解和识别目标导向智能体提供了形式化工具。

Abstract: Structural causal models (SCMs) were conceived to formulate and answer causal questions. This paper shows that SCMs can also be used to formulate and answer teleological questions, concerning the intentions of a state-aware, goal-directed agent intervening in a causal system. We review limitations of previous approaches to modeling such agents, and then introduce intentional interventions, a new time-agnostic operator that induces a twin SCM we call a structural final model (SFM). SFMs treat observed values as the outcome of intentional interventions and relate them to the counterfactual conditions of those interventions (what would have happened had the agent not intervened). We show how SFMs can be used to empirically detect agents and to discover their intentions.

</details>


### [52] [Evaluating Game Difficulty in Tetris Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18994)
*Chun-Jui Wang,Jian-Ting Guo,Hung Guei,Chung-Chin Shih,Ti-Rong Wu,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用随机Gumbel AlphaZero (SGAZ)作为评估器，研究了俄罗斯方块益智游戏的难度评估问题，评估了持有方块、预览方块数量及新增方块变体对游戏难度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Tetris Block Puzzle及其变体拥有数千万下载量，但目前缺乏对不同规则集难度进行原则性评估的方法。之前有研究使用AlphaZero作为国际象棋变体的强评估器，但尚未应用于随机益智游戏领域。

Method: 采用随机Gumbel AlphaZero (SGAZ)作为预算感知的规划智能体，在8x8网格上评估规则变更。评估指标包括训练奖励和收敛迭代次数。测试的规则变更包括：持有块数量h、预览持有块数量p，以及新增Tetris块变体。

Result: 增加h和p会降低难度（更高奖励和更快收敛）；添加更多Tetris块变体会增加难度，其中T-pentomino（五连方块）导致最大的减速效果。SGAZ在较小模拟预算下就能提供强劲表现。

Conclusion: SGAZ能够在小规模模拟预算下实现强大游戏表现，实现跨规则集的高效、可重复比较，为随机益智游戏的未来设计提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Tetris Block Puzzle is a single player stochastic puzzle in which a player places blocks on an 8 x 8 grid to complete lines; its popular variants have amassed tens of millions of downloads. Despite this reach, there is little principled assessment of which rule sets are more difficult. Inspired by prior work that uses AlphaZero as a strong evaluator for chess variants, we study difficulty in this domain using Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero (SGAZ), a budget-aware planning agent for stochastic environments. We evaluate rule changes including holding block h, preview holding block p, and additional Tetris block variants using metrics such as training reward and convergence iterations. Empirically, increasing h and p reduces difficulty (higher reward and faster convergence), while adding more Tetris block variants increases difficulty, with the T-pentomino producing the largest slowdown. Through analysis, SGAZ delivers strong play under small simulation budgets, enabling efficient, reproducible comparisons across rule sets and providing a reference for future design in stochastic puzzle games.

</details>


### [53] [Regret Bounds for Competitive Resource Allocation with Endogenous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18999)
*Rui Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究N个交互模块在T轮中的在线资源分配问题，与标准在线优化不同之处在于成本是内生的——通过编码成对合作与竞争关系的交互矩阵W依赖于完整分配向量。研究分析了三种范式：均匀分配（成本未知）、门控分配（成本估计）和竞争性分配（乘数权重更新，成本揭示）。在对抗性序列下，竞争性分配达到O(√(T log N))遗憾界，显著优于均匀分配的Ω(T)和门控分配的O(T^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线资源分配中成本内生性问题。传统在线优化假设成本仅依赖于当前决策，但实际中模块间存在交互，成本取决于完整分配向量。这种内生成本给在线学习带来新挑战，需要设计能够利用交互反馈揭示成本信息的算法。

Method: 采用遗憾分析框架，对比三种分配范式：(1)均匀分配不考虑成本信息；(2)门控分配需先估计成本；(3)竞争性分配使用乘数权重更新(MWU)直接利用交互反馈揭示的内生成本信息。同时分析交互拓扑W的计算-遗憾权衡：全连接图、稀疏图和环结构（包括五行拓扑）。

Result: 主要结果：在对抗性序列下，三种范式产生严格分离的遗憾界——均匀分配Ω(T)、门控分配O(T^{2/3})、竞争性分配O(√(T log N))。拓扑方面，全连接(|E|=O(N^2))遗憾界最紧但计算成本O(N^2)；稀疏拓扑(|E|=O(N))遗憾增加O(√(log N))但计算成本降至O(N)；环结构（含五行拓扑）最小化计算×遗憾乘积。

Conclusion: 首次为模块化架构中分散竞争性分配提供了形式化的遗憾理论依据，确立成本内生性是区别于部分可观测性的根本挑战。五行拓扑作为合作与竞争链接的典型环结构，在计算效率与算法性能间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation among N interacting modules over T rounds. Unlike standard online optimization, costs are endogenous: they depend on the full allocation vector through an interaction matrix W encoding pairwise cooperation and competition.
  We analyze three paradigms: (I) uniform allocation (cost-ignorant), (II) gated allocation (cost-estimating), and (III) competitive allocation via multiplicative weights update with interaction feedback (cost-revealing). Our main results establish a strict separation under adversarial sequences with bounded variation: uniform incurs Omega(T) regret, gated achieves O(T^{2/3}), and competitive achieves O(sqrt(T log N)). The performance gap stems from competitive allocation's ability to exploit endogenous cost information revealed through interactions.
  We further show that W's topology governs a computation-regret tradeoff. Full interaction (|E|=O(N^2)) yields the tightest bound but highest per-step cost, while sparse topologies (|E|=O(N)) increase regret by at most O(sqrt(log N)) while reducing per-step cost from O(N^2) to O(N). Ring-structured topologies with both cooperative and competitive links - of which the five-element Wuxing topology is canonical - minimize the computation x regret product.
  These results provide the first formal regret-theoretic justification for decentralized competitive allocation in modular architectures and establish cost endogeneity as a fundamental challenge distinct from partial observability.
  Keywords: online learning, regret bounds, resource allocation, endogenous costs, interaction topology, multiplicative weights, modular systems, Wuxing topology

</details>


### [54] [Behavioral Fingerprints for LLM Endpoint Stability and Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19022)
*Jonah Leshin,Manish Shah,Ian Timmis,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Stability Monitor is a black-box system that monitors AI model endpoint stability by periodically fingerprinting outputs from a fixed prompt set and detecting distribution shifts using energy distance statistics and permutation tests, addressing the limitation that traditional metrics miss behavioral changes in AI APIs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional reliability metrics (uptime, latency, throughput) fail to capture behavioral changes in AI model endpoints. An endpoint can appear "healthy" while its effective model identity changes due to updates to weights, tokenizers, quantization, inference engines, kernels, caching, routing, or hardware. This creates a need for monitoring behavioral consistency of AI-native applications.

Method: The system uses a black-box fingerprinting approach: (1) Maintains a fixed prompt set to sample outputs over time; (2) Compares fingerprints using summed energy distance statistic across prompts; (3) Employs permutation-test p-values as evidence of distribution shift; (4) Aggregates p-values sequentially to detect change events and define stability periods.

Result: In controlled validation, Stability Monitor successfully detects changes to model family, version, inference stack, quantization, and behavioral parameters. In real-world monitoring of the same model across multiple providers, substantial provider-to-provider and within-provider stability differences were observed.

Conclusion: Stability Monitor provides a practical solution for detecting behavioral changes in AI model endpoints. The system successfully identifies changes across multiple dimensions (model family, version, quantization, etc.) and reveals significant stability variations across providers. This approach fills a critical gap in AI reliability monitoring by focusing on behavioral consistency rather than traditional uptime metrics.

Abstract: The consistency of AI-native applications depends on the behavioral consistency of the model endpoints that power them. Traditional reliability metrics such as uptime, latency and throughput do not capture behavioral change, and an endpoint can remain "healthy" while its effective model identity changes due to updates to weights, tokenizers, quantization, inference engines, kernels, caching, routing, or hardware. We introduce Stability Monitor, a black-box stability monitoring system that periodically fingerprints an endpoint by sampling outputs from a fixed prompt set and comparing the resulting output distributions over time. Fingerprints are compared using a summed energy distance statistic across prompts, with permutation-test p-values as evidence of distribution shift aggregated sequentially to detect change events and define stability periods. In controlled validation, Stability Monitor detects changes to model family, version, inference stack, quantization, and behavioral parameters. In real-world monitoring of the same model hosted by multiple providers, we observe substantial provider-to-provider and within-provider stability differences.

</details>


### [55] [Man and machine: artificial intelligence and judicial decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19042)
*Arthur Dyevre,Ahmad Shahvaroughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综合综述，探讨人工智能在司法决策（审前、量刑和假释）中的应用，聚焦于风险评估工具，从AI工具性能与公平性、法官偏见与优势、AI+人类交互三个维度展开分析，发现现有实证证据表明AI决策辅助工具的影响较为有限或不存在，并呼吁跨学科整合研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术在司法决策中的应用引发了对透明度、可靠性和问责制的担忧，同时凸显了人类判断的局限性，需要理解法官如何与AI决策辅助工具交互

Method: 综合综述方法，连接计算机科学、经济学、法律、犯罪学和心理学四个领域的研究成果

Result: 现有实证证据表明，AI决策辅助工具对审前和量刑决策的影响较为温和或不存在；研究已在评估自动风险评估工具的预测有效性、记录司法决策偏见等方面取得进展，但对法官如何使用算法建议的研究较为有限

Conclusion: 需要进一步研究评估AI风险评估工具的性能、理解法官如何在嘈杂决策环境中导航以及个体特征如何影响法官对AI建议的反应；AI与人类的比较研究具有揭示算法工具和人类决策者新见解的潜力，倡导未来研究加强跨学科整合

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies into judicial decision-making - particularly in pretrial, sentencing, and parole contexts - has generated substantial concerns about transparency, reliability, and accountability. At the same time, these developments have brought the limitations of human judgment into sharper relief and underscored the importance of understanding how judges interact with AI-based decision aids. Using criminal justice risk assessment as a focal case, we conduct a synthetic review connecting three intertwined aspects of AI's role in judicial decision-making: the performance and fairness of AI tools, the strengths and biases of human judges, and the nature of AI+human interactions. Across the fields of computer science, economics, law, criminology and psychology, researchers have made significant progress in evaluating the predictive validity of automated risk assessment instruments, documenting biases in judicial decision-making, and, to a more limited extent, examining how judges use algorithmic recommendations. While the existing empirical evidence indicates that the impact of AI decision aid tools on pretrial and sentencing decisions is modest or inexistent, our review also reveals important gaps in the canvassed literatures. Further research is needed to evaluate the performance of AI risk assessment instruments, understand how judges navigate noisy decision making environments and how individual characteristics influence judges' responses to AI advice. We argue that AI vs Human comparisons have the potential to yield new insights into both algorithmic tools and human decision-makers and advocate greater interdisciplinary integration and cross-fertilization in future research.

</details>


### [56] [Serendipity by Design: Evaluating the Impact of Cross-domain Mappings on Human and LLM Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19087)
*Qiawen Ella Liu,Marina Dubova,Henry Conklin,Takumi Harada,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大型语言模型(LLM)是否像人类一样具有创造力，以及跨域映射干预是否能在两者中提升创造力。人类参与者和LLM为10种日常产品生成新颖特性，比较两种提示条件：跨域映射(从随机远程源域迁移特征)和用户需求(针对未满足用户需求的创新)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否以与人类相同的方式具有创造力，以及相同的创造力干预措施是否对两者都有效。

Method: 实验设计：10种日常产品(如背包、电视)，两种提示条件：(i)跨域映射-要求从随机分配的源域(如章鱼、仙人掌、GPS)迁移特征；(ii)用户需求-提出针对未满足用户需求的创新。比较人类参与者与LLM的创造力表现。

Result: 人类参与者从随机分配的跨域映射中可靠地受益；LLM平均生成的想法比人类更原创，但跨域映射没有显示统计学上显著的效果。然而，在两种系统中，当灵感来源与目标语义距离更远时，跨域映射的影响增加。

Conclusion: 结果突出了远程联想在创造性 ideation 中的作用，以及人类和LLM对相同创造力干预措施的反应存在系统性差异。

Abstract: Are large language models (LLMs) creative in the same way humans are, and can the same interventions increase creativity in both? We evaluate a promising but largely untested intervention for creativity: forcing creators to draw an analogy from a random, remote source domain (''cross-domain mapping''). Human participants and LLMs generated novel features for ten daily products (e.g., backpack, TV) under two prompts: (i) cross-domain mapping, which required translating a property from a randomly assigned source (e.g., octopus, cactus, GPS), and (ii) user-need, which required proposing innovations targeting unmet user needs. We show that humans reliably benefit from randomly assigned cross-domain mappings, while LLMs, on average, generate more original ideas than humans and do not show a statistically significant effect of cross-domain mappings. However, in both systems, the impact of cross-domain mapping increases when the inspiration source becomes more semantically distant from the target. Our results highlight both the role of remote association in creative ideation and systematic differences in how humans and LLMs respond to the same intervention for creativity.

</details>


### [57] [LuMamba: Latent Unified Mamba for Electrode Topology-Invariant and Efficient EEG Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19100)
*Danaé Broustail,Anna Tegon,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Yawei Li,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LuMamba是一个用于EEG的自监督基础模型，通过结合拓扑不变编码与线性复杂度状态空间建模来解决EEG领域的基础模型构建难题。该模型仅需4.6M参数即可在多个下游任务上达到先进性能，且计算效率比现有模型高377倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建EEG基础模型面临两大核心挑战：1) 不同电极拓扑导致的数据异构性；2) Transformer架构的二次复杂度带来的计算可扩展性问题。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出LuMamba框架，整合以下技术：1) 使用LUNA的查询学习交叉注意力机制实现通道统一，生成拓扑不变编码；2) 采用FEMBA的双向Mamba块进行高效时序建模；3) 首次系统研究LeJEPA（潜在-欧几里得联合嵌入预测架构）在生物信号学习中的应用。预训练数据来自TUEG语料库的21,000+小时无标签EEG。

Result: 1) 单独使用掩码重构产生结构化但泛化性较差的表征；2) 单独使用LeJEPA产生弥散的嵌入；3) 两者结合获得最鲁棒性能。模型在TUAB上达到80.99%平衡准确率，阿尔茨海默病检测达到0.97 AUPR。

Conclusion: LuMamba成功实现了高效且鲁棒的EEG基础模型构建，在仅4.6M参数条件下达到先进性能，且计算效率显著优于Transformer架构（377×更少FLOPS），可扩展至12×更长的序列长度。这项工作为生物信号领域的自监督学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) enables non-invasive monitoring of brain activity across clinical and neurotechnology applications, yet building foundation models for EEG remains challenging due to \emph{differing electrode topologies} and \emph{computational scalability}, as Transformer architectures incur quadratic sequence complexity. As a joint solution, we propose \textbf{LuMamba} (\textbf{L}atent \textbf{U}nified \textbf{Mamba}), a self-supervised framework combining topology-invariant encodings with linear-complexity state-space modeling, using LUNA's learned-query cross-attention mechanism for channel unification~\cite{luna}, and FEMBA's bidirectional Mamba blocks for efficient temporal modeling~\cite{femba}. Within this architecture, we provide the first systematic investigation of the Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (LeJEPA) for biosignal learning. Pre-trained on over 21,000 hours of unlabeled EEG from the TUEG corpus, LuMamba is evaluated on five downstream tasks spanning abnormality detection, artifact recognition, and mental condition classification across electrode configurations ranging from 16 to 26 channels. In the pre-training objective, masked reconstruction alone yields structured but less generalizable representations, while LeJEPA alone produces diffuse embeddings; combining both objectives achieves the most robust performance. With only 4.6M parameters, LuMamba attains 80.99\% balanced accuracy on TUAB and achieves state-of-art performance on Alzheimer's detection (0.97 AUPR), while requiring \textbf{377$\times$ fewer FLOPS} than state-of-art models at equivalent sequence lengths and scaling to \textbf{12$\times$ longer sequences} before reaching typical GPU memory limits. Code is available at https://github.com/pulp-bio/biofoundation

</details>


### [58] [How Uncertainty Estimation Scales with Sampling in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19118)
*Maksym Del,Markus Kängsepp,Marharyta Domnich,Ardi Tampuu,Lisa Yankovskaya,Meelis Kull,Mark Fishel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展思维链推理场景下推理语言模型的不确定性估计问题。通过并行采样方法，结合口头化置信度和自一致性两种信号，在三个推理模型和17个任务（涵盖数学、STEM和人文领域）上进行了系统性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署推理语言模型时，不确定性估计至关重要，但在扩展思维链推理场景下对此的理解仍然不足。需要一种完全黑盒的方法来估计不确定性。

Method: 使用并行采样作为完全黑盒的方法，结合口头化置信度（verbalized confidence）和自一致性（self-consistency）两种信号，在三个推理模型和17个任务上进行实验，涵盖数学、STEM和人文领域。

Result: 自一致性和口头化置信度在推理模型中都能扩展，但自一致性初始区分度较低，在中等采样预算下落后于口头化置信度。仅用两个样本的混合估计器就能将AUROC平均提升+12%，且优于单独使用任一信号即使扩展到更大预算的效果，之后收益递减。这些效果与领域相关：在数学领域（RLVR风格后训练的原生领域），推理模型获得更高质量的不确定性，表现出更强的互补性和更快的扩展速度。

Conclusion: 信号组合是提升不确定性估计的关键，仅需少量样本即可获得显著增益；数学领域由于是后训练的原生领域，表现最优，STEM和人文领域相对较弱。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying reasoning language models, yet remains poorly understood under extended chain-of-thought reasoning. We study parallel sampling as a fully black-box approach using verbalized confidence and self-consistency. Across three reasoning models and 17 tasks spanning mathematics, STEM, and humanities, we characterize how these signals scale.
  Both self-consistency and verbalized confidence scale in reasoning models, but self-consistency exhibits lower initial discrimination and lags behind verbalized confidence under moderate sampling. Most uncertainty gains, however, arise from signal combination: with just two samples, a hybrid estimator improves AUROC by up to $+12$ on average and already outperforms either signal alone even when scaled to much larger budgets, after which returns diminish. These effects are domain-dependent: in mathematics, the native domain of RLVR-style post-training, reasoning models achieve higher uncertainty quality and exhibit both stronger complementarity and faster scaling than in STEM or humanities.

</details>


### [59] [D5P4: Partition Determinantal Point Process for Diversity in Parallel Discrete Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19146)
*Jonathan Lys,Vincent Gripon,Bastien Pasdeloup,Axel Marmoret,Lukas Mauch,Fabien Cardinaux,Ghouthi Boukli Hacene*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对离散扩散模型的广义束搜索框架(D5P4)，通过DPP进行MAP推理实现多样性与概率的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解码方法（如束搜索）不适用于迭代去噪，现有的扩散解码技术对批量内多样性控制有限

Method: 1. 提出广义束搜索框架，并行生成候选，支持模块化束选择目标；2. D5P4将选择步骤表述为DPP上的MAP推理；3. 使用可扩展的贪婪求解器，保持多GPU兼容性

Result: 在自由形式生成和问答任务上，D5P4相比强基线提升了多样性，同时保持了具有竞争力的生成质量

Conclusion: D5P4实现了模型概率与目标多样性之间的显式权衡，且计算开销接近零

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are promising alternatives to autoregressive approaches for text generation, yet their decoding methods remain under-studied. Standard decoding methods for autoregressive models, such as beam search, do not directly apply to iterative denoising, and existing diffusion decoding techniques provide limited control over in-batch diversity. To bridge this gap, we introduce a generalized beam-search framework for discrete diffusion that generates candidates in parallel and supports modular beam-selection objectives. As a diversity-focused instantiation, we propose D5P4, which formulates the selection step as MAP inference over a Determinantal Point Process. Leveraging a scalable greedy solver, D5P4 maintains multi-GPU compatibility and enables an explicit trade-off between model probability and target diversity with near-zero compute overhead. Experiments on free-form generation and question answering demonstrate that D5P4 improves diversity over strong baselines while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [60] [Box Maze: A Process-Control Architecture for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19182)
*Zou Qiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Box Maze框架，通过将LLM推理分解为三个显式层（记忆接地、结构化推理和边界 enforcement）来提高推理可靠性。初步仿真结果表明，该架构将对抗条件下边界失败率从约40%降至1%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在对抗性提示下容易产生幻觉和不可靠推理，现有安全方法（如RLHF和输出过滤）主要在行为层面操作，缺乏明确的推理过程完整性架构机制。

Method: Box Maze框架包含三个认知控制层：记忆接地、结构化推理和边界 enforcement。针对多个异构LLM系统（DeepSeek-V3、Doubao、Qwen）进行n=50个对抗场景的仿真评估。

Result: 显式认知控制层可改善边界维护一致性，架构约束将对抗条件下边界失败率从约40%（基线RLHF）降至1%以下。

Conclusion: 过程级控制可能是提高大型语言模型可靠性的有前景方向，但当前验证仍基于仿真，需进一步实证研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities but remain vulnerable to hallucination and unreliable reasoning under adversarial prompting. Existing safety approaches -- such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and output filtering -- primarily operate at the behavioral level and may lack explicit architectural mechanisms for enforcing reasoning process integrity.
  This paper proposes the Box Maze framework, a conceptual process-control architecture that decomposes LLM reasoning into three explicit layers: memory grounding, structured inference, and boundary enforcement. We introduce preliminary simulation-based evaluation involving progressive boundary erosion scenarios across multiple heterogeneous LLM systems (DeepSeek-V3, Doubao, Qwen). Results from n=50 adversarial scenarios suggest that explicit cognitive control layers may improve consistency in boundary maintenance, with architectural constraints reducing boundary failure rates from approximately 40% (baseline RLHF) to below 1% under adversarial conditions.
  While current validation is simulation-based, these preliminary results indicate that process-level control may offer a promising direction for improving reliability in large language model reasoning.

</details>


### [61] [OS-Themis: A Scalable Critic Framework for Generalist GUI Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19191)
*Zehao Li,Zhenyu Wu,Yibo Zhao,Bowen Yang,Jingjing Xie,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Kaiming Jin,Jianze Liang,Zonglin Li,Feng Wu,Bowen Zhou,Zun Wang,Zichen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OS-Themis是一个用于GUI智能体训练的多智能体评判框架，通过里程碑分解和审查机制实现准确、可扩展的奖励评估，在AndroidWorld上相比现有方法有显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）有潜力提高GUI智能体在随机环境中的鲁棒性，但训练对奖励函数的质量高度敏感。现有奖励方法难以同时实现可扩展性和高性能。

Method: OS-Themis采用多智能体评判框架，将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑（milestones）以隔离关键决策证据，并引入审查机制（review mechanism）在最终判决前严格审计证据链。评价部分引入OmniGUIRewardBench（OGRBench）——一个跨平台的综合基准测试。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上的广泛实验表明，使用OS-Themis支持在线RL训练可获得10.3%的提升，在自训练循环中用于轨迹验证和过滤可获得6.9%的提升。所有评估的模型在OS-Themis下都达到了最佳性能。

Conclusion: OS-Themis作为可扩展且准确的多智能体评判框架，能有效提升GUI智能体的训练效果。通过将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑并引入审查机制，该方法解决了现有奖励函数难以同时兼顾可扩展性和性能的问题。OGRBench基准的引入为GUI结果奖励的评估提供了标准化测试平台。实验表明，OS-Themis在在线RL训练中提升了10.3%的性能，在自训练循环的轨迹验证和过滤中提升了6.9%，具有推动智能体进化的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to improve the robustness of GUI agents in stochastic environments, yet training is highly sensitive to the quality of the reward function. Existing reward approaches struggle to achieve both scalability and performance. To address this, we propose OS-Themis, a scalable and accurate multi-agent critic framework. Unlike a single judge, OS-Themis decomposes trajectories into verifiable milestones to isolate critical evidence for decision making and employs a review mechanism to strictly audit the evidence chain before making the final verdict. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce OmniGUIRewardBench (OGRBench), a holistic cross-platform benchmark for GUI outcome rewards, where all evaluated models achieve their best performance under OS-Themis. Extensive experiments on AndroidWorld show that OS-Themis yields a 10.3% improvement when used to support online RL training, and a 6.9% gain when used for trajectory validation and filtering in the self-training loop, highlighting its potential to drive agent evolution.

</details>

### [62] [QuaQue: Design and SQL Implementation of Condensed Algebra for Concurrent Versioning of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18654)
*Jey Puget Gil,Emmanuel Coquery,John Samuel,Gilles Gesquière*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuaQue是ConVer-G系统的核心组件，通过将SPARQL查询翻译为SQL，实现对版本化知识图谱的高效查询。该方法采用新型压缩代数(condensed algebra)，在关系模型中利用位串紧凑存储版本信息，在标准关系数据库系统中实现并发版本的高效查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本化知识图谱的查询管理面临重大挑战，特别是在多版本数据的高效查询方面。现有方法难以在标准关系数据库系统中有效处理并发版本的SPARQL查询。

Method: 提出压缩代数(condensed algebra)，在关系模型中使用位串紧凑存储版本信息，将SPARQL代数翻译为SQL，实现对版本化知识图谱的查询。

Result: 与原生RDF三元组存储进行对比基准测试，验证了该方法的可行性和性能优势。

Conclusion: 基于压缩代数的关系模型方法能够在标准关系数据库系统中有效支持版本化知识图谱的SPARQL查询，具有良好的性能表现。

Abstract: The management of versioned knowledge graphs presents significant challenges, particularly in querying data across multiple versions efficiently. This paper introduces QuaQue, a key component of the ConVer-G system, which addresses this challenge by translating SPARQL (SPARQL Protocol and RDF Query Language) queries into SQL (Structured Query Language). QuaQue leverages a novel condensed algebra to operate on a relational model where versioning information is compactly stored using bitstrings. This approach allows for efficient querying of concurrent versions of knowledge graphs within a standard relational database system. We present the key concepts of our condensed algebra, detail the translation process from SPARQL algebra to SQL, and provide a comparative benchmark against a native RDF (Resource Description Framework) triple store, demonstrating the viability and performance benefits of our approach.

</details>


### [63] [Let's Play Tag: Linear Time Evaluation of Conjunctive Queries under TGD Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18709)
*Nofar Carmeli,Carsten Lutz,Marcin Przybyłko*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 研究在线性时间内评估带Tuple-Generating Dependencies (TGDs)约束的合取查询的极限，涵盖多种评估模式：单测试、全测试、计数、字典序直接访问和枚举。对于两类TGDs（非递归TGDs和前哨保护完整TGDs），可将对无约束情况的已知二分法进行提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带约束的查询评估 dichotomy（复杂性二分）问题，填补当前分类理论在约束设置下的空白

Method: 提出一种提升方法，将无约束设置中已知的复杂性 dichotomy 结果推广到特定的约束TGDs类

Result: 成功将 dichotomy 结果推广到单测试、全测试、计数和字典序直接访问模式；对于非递归TGDs和前哨保护完整TGDs类别实现了提升

Conclusion: 对于枚举模式和更宽松的TGDs类别仍存在挑战，需要进一步研究

Abstract: We study the limits of linear time evaluation of conjunctive queries under constraints expressed as tuple-generating dependencies (TGDs), across several modes of query evaluation: single-testing, all-testing, counting, lexicographic direct access, and enumeration. While full classifications seem far beyond reach, we propose an approach that, for some evaluation modes and classes of TGDs, makes it possible to lift known dichotomies from the unconstrained setting. In particular, our approach applies to all mentioned evaluation modes except enumeration, when the constraints fall into one of two classes: non-recursive sets of TGDs in which every TGD uses at most binary relation symbols in the head or has at most two frontier variables; and frontier-guarded full TGDs. We further provide a collection of examples showcasing the challenges that arise for enumeration and for less restrictive classes of TGDs.

</details>

### [64] [Can LLMs Reason Like Automated Theorem Provers for Rust Verification? VCoT-Bench: Evaluating via Verification Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18334)
*Zichen Xie,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VCoT-Lift是一个框架,可以将低级求解器推理提升为高级、可读的验证步骤,从而实现对LLMs在Rust程序验证中逻辑推理能力的细粒度评估。基于此框架构建了VCoT-Bench基准,包含1,988个VCoT完成任务,从三个正交维度评估LLMs的验证理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将Rust验证视为黑箱,仅通过证明提示的二进制通过/失败结果来评估模型,这模糊了模型是否真正理解验证非平凡Rust代码所需的逻辑推理。为填补这一空白,需要一种细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 提出VCoT-Lift框架,将求解器级推理显式暴露为验证思维链(Verification Chain-of-Thought),从而提供具体的事实真相用于细粒度评估。基于该框架构建VCoT-Bench基准测试。

Result: 对10个最先进模型的评估揭示了严重的脆弱性,表明当前LLMs在推理能力方面远不及自动定理证明器所展现的水平。

Conclusion: VCoT-Lift和VCoT-Bench为评估LLMs在Rust验证中的推理能力提供了新的方法论和基准,揭示了当前模型的局限性,为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly assist secure software development, their ability to meet the rigorous demands of Rust program verification remains unclear. Existing evaluations treat Rust verification as a black box, assessing models only by binary pass or fail outcomes for proof hints. This obscures whether models truly understand the logical deductions required for verifying nontrivial Rust code. To bridge this gap, we introduce VCoT-Lift, a framework that lifts low-level solver reasoning into high-level, human-readable verification steps. By exposing solver-level reasoning as an explicit Verification Chain-of-Thought, VCoT-Lift provides a concrete ground truth for fine-grained evaluation. Leveraging VCoT-Lift, we introduce VCoT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,988 VCoT completion tasks for rigorously evaluating LLMs' understanding of the entire verification process. VCoT-Bench measures performance along three orthogonal dimensions: robustness to varying degrees of missing proofs, competence across different proof types, and sensitivity to the proof locations. Evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals severe fragility, indicating that current LLMs fall well short of the reasoning capabilities exhibited by automated theorem provers.

</details>


### [65] [Where are the Hidden Gems? Applying Transformer Models for Design Discussion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18393)
*Lawrence Arkoh,Daniel Feitosa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer模型（BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M、ChatGPT-4o-mini）在跨领域检测软件设计决策讨论中的表现，在Stack Overflow上训练并在GitHub工件上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计决策是软件工程的核心，出现在Q&A论坛、邮件列表、PR、issue和提交信息中。机器学习技术已用于检测设计决策，但标记数据稀缺且注释成本高。传统分类器的跨域策略已有成功案例，但Transformer模型在此设置中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 对五种Transformer模型进行概念复制研究：在Stack Overflow上微调，在GitHub工件（PR、issue、commit）上评估。同时测试了相似词注入数据增强方法。

Result: BERT和RoBERTa跨域召回率强；XLNet精确率高但召回率低；ChatGPT-4o-mini召回率最高且整体性能具竞争力；LaMini-Flan-T5-77M作为轻量级替代方案精确率高但性能不够均衡；相似词注入未带来有意义提升。

Conclusion: 结果突出了现代语言模型在检测设计讨论方面的机遇与权衡，为软件工程中的设计决策识别提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Design decisions are at the core of software engineering and appear in Q\&A forums, mailing lists, pull requests, issue trackers, and commit messages. Design discussions spanning a project's history provide valuable information for informed decision-making, such as refactoring and software modernization. Machine learning techniques have been used to detect design decisions in natural language discussions; however, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of labeled data and the high cost of annotation. Prior work adopted cross-domain strategies with traditional classifiers, training on one domain and testing on another. Despite their success, transformer-based models, which often outperform traditional methods, remain largely unexplored in this setting. The goal of this work is to investigate the performance of transformer-based models (i.e., BERT, RoBERTa, XLNet, LaMini-Flan-T5-77M, and ChatGPT-4o-mini) for detecting design-related discussions. To this end, we conduct a conceptual replication of prior cross-domain studies while extending them with modern transformer architectures and addressing methodological issues in earlier work. The models were fine-tuned on Stack Overflow and evaluated on GitHub artifacts (i.e., pull requests, issues, and commits). BERT and RoBERTa show strong recall across domains, while XLNet achieves higher precision but lower recall. ChatGPT-4o-mini yields the highest recall and competitive overall performance, whereas LaMini-Flan-T5-77M provides a lightweight alternative with stronger precision but less balanced performance. We also evaluated similar-word injection for data augmentation, but unlike prior findings, it did not yield meaningful improvements. Overall, these results highlight both the opportunities and trade-offs of using modern language models for detecting design discussion.

</details>


### [66] [SQL-Commenter: Aligning Large Language Models for SQL Comment Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18606)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jia Xu,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Jiajia Ma,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SQL-Commenter是一种基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL自动注释生成方法，通过构建复杂SQL数据集、持续预训练、监督微调和DPO偏好优化，显著提升注释质量，在多项指标上超越Qwen3-14B等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL查询理解困难，复杂语法、多样化连接类型和深度嵌套导致代码可读性差、维护困难、知识传递受限。现有自动化SQL注释生成面临两大挑战：1) 现有数据集不足以代表复杂真实查询；2) 大语言模型对SQL特定语义理解不足，即使经过持续预训练和监督微调后仍难以准确生成注释。

Method: 1) 构建包含复杂SQL查询及专家验证注释的综合数据集；2) 在大型SQL语料上进行持续预训练以增强LLM的语法和语义理解能力；3) 进行监督微调；4) 引入基于人类反馈的直接偏好优化(DPO)，利用偏好损失函数优化输出质量。

Result: 在Spider和Bird基准上评估，SQL-Commenter显著优于SOTA基线模型。相较于最强基线Qwen3-14B，在BLEU-4、METEOR和ROUGE-L上分别提升9.29、4.99和13.23个百分点。人类评估表明生成注释在正确性、完整性和自然性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL-Commenter方法通过构建高质量数据集、持续预训练、监督微调和DPO偏好优化，有效解决了LLM对复杂SQL语义理解不足的问题，实现了高质量SQL注释自动生成。

Abstract: SQL query comprehension is a significant challenge due to complex syntax, diverse join types, and deep nesting. Many queries lack adequate comments, severely hindering code readability, maintainability, and knowledge transfer. Automated SQL comment generation faces two main challenges: limited datasets that inadequately represent complex real-world queries, and Large Language Models' (LLMs) insufficient understanding of SQL-specific semantics. Our empirical analysis shows that even after continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, LLMs struggle with complex SQL semantics, yielding inaccurate comments. To address this, we propose SQL-Commenter, an advanced method based on LLaMA-3.1-8B. We first construct a comprehensive dataset of complex SQL queries with expert-verified comments. Next, we perform continual pre-training on a large SQL corpus to enhance the LLM's syntax and semantic understanding, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Finally, we introduce Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) using human feedback. SQL-Commenter utilizes a preference-based loss function to favor preferred outputs, enhancing fine-grained semantic learning and context-dependent quality assessment. Evaluated on the Spider and Bird benchmarks, SQL-Commenter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On average, it surpasses the strongest baseline (Qwen3-14B) by 9.29, 4.99, and 13.23 percentage points on BLEU-4, METEOR, and ROUGE-L, respectively. Moreover, human evaluation demonstrates the superior quality of comments generated by SQL-Commenter in terms of correctness, completeness, and naturalness.

</details>


### [67] [Green Architectural Tactics in ML-enabled Systems: An LLM-based Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18734)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Silverio Martínez-Fernández,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了开源机器学习项目中绿色AI可持续实践的实施情况，通过对205个GitHub项目进行软件仓库挖掘，并设计了一种基于大语言模型的新机制来识别已知和新的可持续实践，发现文献中报告的绿色策略在实践中确实被使用，同时揭示了9个先前未记录的可持续实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和人工智能技术的采用增加引发了对环境可持续性的担忧。开发和部署ML系统需要大量计算资源，尤其是训练和推理阶段。虽然先前研究提出了可持续实践目录（即绿色策略），但对其在实践中的采用情况以及是否存在其他未记录的策略缺乏了解。

Method: 对GitHub上的205个开源ML项目进行软件仓库挖掘研究。为支持分析，设计了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的新机制，能够从代码仓库中识别已知和新的可持续实践。

Result: 研究结果证实了文献中报告的绿色策略在实践中被使用，尽管采用率有所不同。此外，基于LLM的方法揭示了9个先前未记录的可持续实践。每个策略都配有代码示例以帮助采用和集成。

Conclusion: 为寻求减少ML系统环境影响的从业者提供见解，并为未来自动化检测和采用可持续实践的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Context: The increasing adoption of machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies raises growing concerns about their environmental sustainability. Developing and deploying ML-enabled systems is computationally intensive, particularly during training and inference. Green AI has emerged to address these issues by promoting efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. While prior research has proposed catalogs of sustainable practices (i.e., green tactics), there remains limited understanding of their adoption in practice and whether additional, undocumented tactics exist. Objective: This study aims to investigate the extent to which existing sustainable practices are implemented in real-world ML-enabled systems and to identify previously undocumented practices that support environmental sustainability. Method: We conduct a mining software repository study on 205 open-source ML projects on GitHub. To support our analysis, we design a novel mechanism based on large language models (LLMs) capable of identifying both known and new sustainable practices from code repositories. Results: Our findings confirm that green tactics reported in the literature are used in practice, although adoption rates vary. Furthermore, our LLM-based approach reveals nine previously undocumented sustainable practices. Each tactic is supported with code examples to aid adoption and integration. Conclusions: We finally provide insights for practitioners seeking to reduce the environmental impact of ML-enabled systems and offer a foundation for future research in automating the detection and adoption of sustainable practices.

</details>


### [68] [SpaceTime Programming: Live and Omniscient Exploration of Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18735)
*Jean-Baptiste Döderlein,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher,Benoit Combemale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpaceTime Programming（时空编程）范式，通过统一的trace机制同时捕获程序执行状态和代码变化，使开发者能够在空间（代码变体）和时间（执行流程）两个维度上探索程序。作为概念验证，实现了SpaceTimePy Python库，并构建了live omniscient debugger和Pygame游戏开发工具。在5个真实Python项目上评估，性能开销为35%-150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程环境将静态代码与动态执行分离，开发者需在编写代码和观察执行之间切换，缺乏有效工具理解代码变更与运行时行为之间的关系。现有方案（探索式编程、live编程、omniscient调试）各自聚焦于特定方面，缺乏整合。

Method: 提出SpaceTime Programming范式，统一探索式编程、live编程和omniscient调试。核心是trace机制，不仅捕获执行状态，还捕获对应的代码变更。实现SpaceTimePy Python库支持该范式。

Result: 1）实现live omniscient debugger和Pygame游戏开发工具（Flappy Bird类游戏）；2）在5个真实Python项目上评估，测试套件性能开销35%-150%。

Conclusion: SpaceTime Programming成功统一了三种编程范式，创建了同时支持代码变体探索（空间）和执行流探索（时间）的编程模型，但存在较高的性能开销（35%-150%）。

Abstract: Programming environments typically separate the world of static code from the dynamic execution of programs. Developers must switch between writing code and observing its execution, often with limited tools to understand the relationship between code changes and runtime behavior. Several paradigms and approaches exist to bridge this gap, including exploratory programming for comparing code variants, live programming for immediate feedback, and omniscient debugging for exploring execution history. However, existing solutions tend to focus on specific aspects and one specific paradigm rather than providing a fully integrated environment with multiple capabilities. This paper introduces \spacetime Programming, a novel approach that unifies these paradigms to create a programming model for exploring both code modifications and execution flow. At the core of our approach is a trace mechanism that captures not only execution state but also the corresponding code changes, enabling developers to explore programs in both space (code variants) and time (execution flow). As a proof of concept, we implemented a Python library supporting SpaceTime Programming and applied it in two contexts: a live omniscient debugger and a Pygame game development tool, showcased through a Flappy Bird-like game. We further evaluated SpaceTimePy on five real-world Python projects, finding performance overhead ranging from 35% to 150% on test suites.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [From Servers to Sites: Compositional Power Trace Generation of LLM Inference for Infrastructure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18383)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Ram Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM推理工作负载的数据中心电力建模框架，通过将GPU在计算密集型prefill、低功耗decode和idle状态间的转换建模为可组合的两个组件（工作负载驱动的状态转换和配置特定的功率分布），能够从测量轨迹学习并为新流量条件和服务配置合成功率曲线，在多个LLM和GPU配置下实现中位数绝对能量误差低于5%，并保留时间自相关结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心电力模型未能捕捉LLM推理工作负载的特殊性：GPU在计算密集型prefill、低功耗decode和idle状态间快速切换，设施需求取决于这些状态在多设备上的演变和同步方式。运营商需要细粒度轨迹进行资源配置和调度，公用事业需要场地级负载曲线进行容量和互连规划。

Method: 提出可组合的电力表示方法，包含两个核心组件：1）工作负载驱动的运行状态转换；2）各状态内配置特定的功率分布。基于此开发了轨迹生成框架，从测量轨迹学习并为新流量条件和服务配置合成功率曲线，可从GPU服务器聚合到机架、行和设施级别的负载曲线。

Result: 在多个LLM、张量并行设置和GPU代际的测试中，框架对大多数配置实现中位数绝对能量误差低于5%，同时保留了时间自相关结构。结果轨迹支持下游分析，包括超额订阅、电力调制和面向公用事业的负载特征分析。

Conclusion: 该框架能够支持基础设施评估，超越了扁平额定功率假设和静态轨迹重放无法支持的分析场景，为LLM推理阶段的数据中心电力管理和规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Datacenter operators and electrical utilities rely on power traces at different spatiotemporal scales. Operators use fine-grained traces for provisioning, facility management, and scheduling, while utilities use site-level load profiles for capacity and interconnection planning. Existing datacenter power models do not capture LLM inference workloads, in which GPUs shift rapidly among compute-intensive prefill, lower-power decode, and idle states, and facility demand depends on how these states evolve and synchronize across many devices. We show that LLM inference power can be represented compositionally through two components: workload-driven transitions among operating states and configuration-specific power distributions within those states. Building on this observation, we develop a trace-generation framework that learns from measured traces and synthesizes power profiles for new traffic conditions and serving configurations. These traces aggregate from GPU servers to rack-, row-, and facility-scale load profiles at the temporal granularity required by the study.
  Across multiple LLMs, tensor-parallel settings, and GPU generations, our framework achieves median absolute energy error below 5% for most configurations while preserving temporal autocorrelation structure. The resulting traces support downstream analyses including oversubscription, power modulation, and utility-facing load characterization, enabling infrastructure evaluations that flat nameplate assumptions and static trace replay cannot support.

</details>


### [70] [High-Performance Portable GPU Primitives for Arbitrary Types and Operators in Julia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18695)
*Emmanuel Pilliat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了KernelForge.jl，一个Julia库，通过两层可移植架构在GPU上实现高性能并行原语（scan、mapreduce、矩阵向量乘法），在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上达到了与CUB和cuBLAS相当或更好的性能，证明了可移植JIT编译抽象可以达到供应商级别的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Kokkos和RAJA等可移植GPU框架在基本并行原语上相比供应商优化库存在可测量的性能开销，作者希望在不牺牲通用性的前提下，通过可移植JIT编译方法达到供应商级别的性能。

Method: 提出了两层可移植架构：1) KernelIntrinsics.jl提供后端无关的warp级shuffle、内存屏障和向量化内存访问抽象；2) KernelForge.jl在此基础上构建高性能算法。Julia的JIT编译特性使得该框架能够在运行时生成针对特定硬件优化的代码。

Result: 在NVIDIA A40上，KernelForge.jl在scan和mapreduce上与CUB内核执行时间相当或更优；在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上，矩阵向量操作与cuBLAS吞吐量相当（大多数配置下）。

Conclusion: 证明了可移植的JIT编译抽象可以在不牺牲通用性的情况下达到供应商级别的吞吐量，为GPU可移植高性能计算提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Portable GPU frameworks such as Kokkos and RAJA reduce the burden of cross-architecture development but typically incur measurable overhead on fundamental parallel primitives relative to vendor-optimized libraries. We present KernelForge.jl, a Julia library that implements scan, mapreduce, and matrix-vector primitives through a two-layer portable architecture: KernelIntrinsics.jl provides backend-agnostic abstractions for warp-level shuffles, memory fences, and vectorized memory access, while KernelForge.jl builds high-performance algorithms exclusively on top of these interfaces. Evaluated on an NVIDIA A40 and an AMD MI300X, KernelForge.jl matches or exceeds CUB kernel execution time on scan and mapreduce on the A40, and matches cuBLAS throughput on matrix-vector operations across most tested configurations-demonstrating, as a proof of concept, that portable JIT-compiled abstractions can achieve vendor-level throughput without sacrificing generality.

</details>


### [71] [Literature Study on Operational Data Analytics Frameworks in Large-scale Computing Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19016)
*Shekhar Suman,Xiaoyu Chu,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于高性能计算(HPC)系统中运维数据分析(ODA)的文献综述研究，旨在提出一个更完整的ODA框架来优化大规模计算基础设施的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据量爆发式增长(2025年每年达泽字节)，现代大规模计算基础设施如HPC系统变得越来越复杂，管理性和可持续性问题日益突出，需要通过细粒度监控和运维数据分析来优化效率。

Method: 本文采用文献研究方法，首先列举了支持ODA能力的大规模计算基础设施的基本支柱，然后研究了在各种环境(主要是HPC)中运行的流行ODA框架，最后基于Sherif Sak提出的分布式图处理生态系统的各层次，提出了一种更完整的ODA框架，该框架扩展了Netti等人提出的现有新型ODA框架的功能。

Result: 提出了一个比现有框架更完整的ODA框架，并与所研究的一些最先进框架进行了比较，突出了其创新性。同时强调了实施先进ODA框架后观察到的显著运维效率提升。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提出了一个更完整的ODA框架，并呼吁更多研究关注这一领域，同时指出了该领域正在进行的Trending研究工作。

Abstract: By 2025, there are zettabytes of data generated every year. The size and complexity of modern large-scale computing infrastructures like High-Performance Computing (HPC) systems continue to evolve and become complex, leaving us wondering about their manageability and sustainability concerns. Because of this reason, those complex systems are provided with fine-grained monitoring and Operational Data Analytics (ODA) capabilities to optimise their efficiency. In this literature study, we list the fundamental pillars of the large-scale computing infrastructures which enable its ODA capabilities, and conduct a study of the popular ODA frameworks operating in various such environments (predominantly HPC). Based on that, we propose a more holistic ODA framework matching the various layers of a large-scale graph-processing distributed ecosystem proposed by Sherif Sak et al, that extends the ODA functionalities presented in an existing novel ODA framework proposed by Netti et al. We compare the holistic ODA framework proposed by us to some of the state-of-the-art frameworks that we study as part of this literature to highlight the novelty, which would hopefully draw more attention to perform extensive research in this field. As part of creating awareness, we highlight the significant operational efficiencies observed as a result of the implementation of the state-of-the-art ODA frameworks to make the study appear beneficial for the readers, and lastly, discuss the trending research work ongoing in this field.

</details>


### [72] [Why Synchronized Time is a Fiction: Daylight Saving Time, Leap Seconds, and the Guillotine Sharpened for Nothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19099)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文认为"存在单一全球时间"这一假设是一个范畴错误（category mistake）。相对论禁止绝对同时性，光速是约定而非测量，量子实验表明自然允许无明确定义时间序列的关联。这些发现对分布式计算有深远影响，揭示了"仅向前时间"（FITO）假设的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现代文明投入大量资源维护"同步时间"（夏令时、闰秒、GPS相对论校正等），但这个假设——存在单一全球时间且时钟可以达成一致——实际上是一个根本性的范畴错误。

Method: 1) 引用Ryle的范畴错误概念；2) 运用狭义/广义相对论证明绝对同时性不可能；3) 指出光速单向是约定而非测量；4) 引用近期因果序实验；5) 追溯对分布式计算的影响（Lamport逻辑时钟、FLP不可能结果、CAP定理）

Result: 相对论否定了绝对同时性；单向光速是约定而非测量结果；量子实验显示存在无确定时间序列的关联；这些在分布式计算中表现为FITO假设及相关的不可能结果。

Conclusion: 夏令时和闰秒不是时间的校正，而是"对不存在的同步的准备的约定"——作者认为当前对时间的调整是在为一个根本不存在的事物做准备。

Abstract: Civilization maintains an elaborate infrastructure devoted to the maintenance of synchronized time. Governments mandate daylight saving time. Standards bodies insert leap seconds into Coordinated Universal Time. Engineers debate leap milliseconds and leap nanoseconds. The Global Positioning System applies relativistic corrections at the nanosecond level. All of these adjustments attempt to preserve an assumption: that a single global time exists and that clocks can be made to agree upon it.
  This paper argues that this assumption constitutes a category mistake in the sense of Ryle (1949). We show that special and general relativity prohibit absolute simultaneity, that the one-way speed of light is conventionally defined rather than measured, and that recent experiments on indefinite causal order demonstrate nature admits correlations with no well-defined temporal sequence. We trace the consequences of this category mistake through distributed computing, where it manifests as the Forward-In-Time-Only (FITO) assumption that underlies Lamport's logical clocks (1978), the impossibility results of Fischer-Lynch-Paterson (1985), and the CAP theorem (2000). From this perspective, daylight saving time and leap seconds are not corrections to time but corrections to conventions -- they sharpen the guillotine of synchronization in preparation for executing something that does not exist.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Frayed RoPE and Long Inputs: A Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18017)
*Davis Wertheimer,Aozhong Zhang,Derrick Liu,Penghang Yin,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时会导致性能崩溃。本文通过经验与理论分析，提出统一的几何视角理解RoPE下注意力机制的行为：注意力会诱导key和query潜在点云的紧密聚类，而RoPE应用于更长输入时会破坏这种聚类分离，导致病态行为。基于此几何视角，提出RoPE-ID方法，通过对部分通道应用高频RoPE使模型能够泛化到更长输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时性能急剧下降的问题。现有分析正确指出长输入会导致通道旋转"超出分布"，但不清楚额外旋转如何导致病态行为。

Method: 1. 经验与理论分析建立几何理解；2. 发现注意力诱导key/query潜在点云的紧密聚类；3. 提出sink tokens概念允许注意力头在不需要时避免token混合；4. 提出RoPE-ID：对部分通道应用高频RoPE

Result: RoPE-ID在LongBench和RULER信息检索基准上，使用1B和3B参数Transformer验证了扩展输入的有效性。

Conclusion: 从几何视角理解RoPE揭示了长输入泛化的根本问题；RoPE-ID作为一种简单修改，无需微调即可使注意力层泛化到更长输入。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a widely adopted technique for encoding position in language models, which, while effective, causes performance breakdown when input length exceeds training length. Prior analyses assert (rightly) that long inputs cause channels to rotate ``out of distribution,'' but it is not clear how extra rotation relates to or causes pathological behavior. Through empirical and theoretical analysis we advance a unified geometric understanding of attention behavior with RoPE. We find that attention induces tight clustering of separated key and query latent point clouds, allowing for creation of sink tokens: placeholders that allow attention heads to avoid token mixing when not required. RoPE applied to longer inputs damages this key/query cluster separation, producing pathological behavior by inhibiting sink token functionality. From this geometric perspective, we propose RoPE-ID (In Distribution), a straightforward modification that allows attention layers to generalize to longer inputs out of the box: apply RoPE with high frequency to a subset of channels. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RoPE-ID for extended inputs using 1B and 3B parameter Transformers on the LongBench and RULER information retrieval benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [Engineering Verifiable Modularity in Transformers via Per-Layer Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18029)
*J. Clayton Kerce*

Main category: cs.LG
